Abstract: LLM agents can fail silently by asserting task completion when the environment state shows otherwise. We study this failure mode, false success, across two agent benchmarks: 9,876 tau2-bench trajectories from 8 model families and 1,879 AppWorld trajectories from 4 model families with text-independent ground truth. False success is common but varies by setting: 45--48% of failures in single-control tau2-bench domains, 3% in dual-control telecom, and 75.8% among AppWorld self-assessing coding-agent trajectories with explicit status claims. LLM judges fail reliably: no configuration across 5 judges, 5 prompt strategies, and full task specifications exceeds AUROC 0.65 on tau2-bench, and the same judges reach only 0.54 AUROC on AppWorld API-call traces. Judges rely on surface completion proxies -- confident closing language in tau2-bench and coarse action-sequence volume in AppWorld -- rather than verified state changes. Lightweight TF-IDF detectors achieve task-disjoint AUROC 0.83 on tau2-bench and 0.95 on AppWorld, recovering 4--8x more false successes than the best judge at the same flag rate with 3,300x lower latency. These results suggest that production monitoring should use lightweight, domain-calibrated detectors as triage signals rather than relying on LLM judges as the primary monitor for false success.

</details>


### [52] [Alignment Collapse Under KV Cache Quantization: Diagnosis and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09864)
*Bruce Changlong Xu,Adarsh Kumarappan,Mu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示低比特KV缓存量化会静默破坏LLM的安全性对齐，提出Per-Channel Reduction (PCR)诊断方法识别三种机制失效模式，实现无需训练的快速对齐恢复（97%恢复率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化评估仅关注困惑度和准确性，未评估安全性影响。研究旨在探索量化如何影响指令微调模型的安全对齐，发现低比特量化可能静默破坏安全对齐，且这种影响被标准指标（困惑度、准确率）掩盖。

Method: 在11个指令微调模型（3.8B-72B）和5个基准（1,894个提示词）上进行评估，发现安全性特征位于低维激活子空间（10²-10³倍更易受量化噪声影响）。提出Per-Channel Reduction (PCR)诊断方法，将模型分为三种机制失效模式：outlier-crushes-safety、outlier-as-safety、multi-layer dilution。PCR使用20个校准提示词进行诊断，无需训练。

Result: Mistral-7B在1.03倍困惑度下丢失15.2%的拒绝率；不存在通用的安全比特宽度；模型特定相变对标准指标不可见。PCR在9个主要模型和1个独立家庭模型上预测正确缓解方向，泛化到未见提示词、模型和生产量化器（包括KIVI达到97.2%恢复率）。训练免费协议仅需约35 GPU分钟，恢复高达97%的对齐损失，最小化内存开销。

Conclusion: 提出的PCR方法成功恢复对齐，解决生产环境中vLLM FP8 KV缓存量化确认的漏洞，为LLM量化部署提供安全性保障。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache quantization is widely used to reduce Large Language Model (LLM) inference memory, yet existing evaluations solely focus on measuring perplexity and accuracy without assessing the safety impact. In this study, we explore alignment preservation under KV cache quantization. Across eleven instruction-tuned models (3.8B-72B) and five benchmarks (1,894 prompts), we find that low-bit quantization can silently destroy safety alignment: Mistral-7B loses 15.2% of its refusals at only 1.03x perplexity, and no universal safe bit-width exists, with sharp model-specific phase transitions invisible to standard metrics. We identify that the root cause is geometric: safety features occupy a low-dimensional activation subspace 10^2-10^3x more vulnerable to quantization noise than the full representation space perplexity averages over. Inspired by this observation, we propose Per-Channel Reduction (PCR), a diagnostic that classifies each model into one of three mechanistic failure modes: outlier-crushes-safety, where safety lives in non-outlier channels collaterally damaged by outlier-driven scale factors; outlier-as-safety, where safety overlaps outlier channels and finer granularity cannot rescue it; and multi-layer dilution, where safety is distributed across many layers and per-layer fixes fail. PCR predicts the correct mitigation direction on all nine primary models and one held-out model from an independent family using 20 calibration prompts. PCR generalizes across unseen prompts, models, and production quantizers, including KIVI with up to 97.2% recovery, succeeding where attention-based allocation methods fail. The resulting training-free protocol, requiring approximately 35 GPU-minutes, recovers up to 97% of lost alignment at minimal memory overhead, addressing vulnerabilities confirmed in production vLLM serving with FP8 KV cache on NVIDIA GPUs.

</details>


### [53] [Calibrating Overconfidence Without Sacrificing Confidence: Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09876)
*Ke Li,Chongzhe Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Feng Liu,Qunli Zhang,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为 Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention (PCHI) 的推理时方法，通过冻结探针检测错误但自信的响应，并条件性地重新缩放注意力头输出来减少语言模型的过自信问题，实现了对不当自信的抑制和对正确自信的保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型经常在错误答案上表现出过高的自信，而标准的校准方法通常全局或基于分数级别操作，在降低不当自信的同时，也可能会损害正确答案上的应有自信。

Method: 提出 Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention (PCHI)，这是一种推理时方法：使用冻结的探针检测可能错误但自信的响应，并在生成置信度时条件性地重新缩放下游注意力头输出。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B-Instruct 解决 OpenMathInstruct 问题时，readout-token PCHI 将 82.2% 的原始错误-yes 置信度读数转换为 "no"，联合干预将 ECE 从 21.9% 降低到 9.2%，仅损坏了 5.1% 的原始正确-yes 置信度读数。Gemma3-4B 上也观察到读出令牌效应。

Conclusion: 可以通过条件应用的内部干预选择性减少语言化的过自信，部分解耦了对不当自信的抑制和对正确自信的损失。

Abstract: Large language models often express high confidence in answers that are wrong. Standard calibration remedies typically act globally or at the score level, reducing unwarranted confidence but also risking erosion of warranted confidence on correct answers. We introduce Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention (PCHI), an inference-time method that uses a frozen probe to detect likely wrong-but-confident responses and conditionally rescales downstream attention-head outputs during confidence generation. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct solving OpenMathInstruct problems with a structured binary confidence field, readout-token PCHI converts 82.2% of originally wrong-yes confidence readouts to $\texttt{no}$, while a joint intervention across upstream confidence-template tokens reduces ECE from 21.9% to 9.2% and damages only 5.1% of originally correct-yes readouts. The readout-token effect also appears on Gemma3-4B, though upstream interventions are weaker and more mask-dependent. These results show that verbalized overconfidence can be selectively reduced through conditionally applied internal intervention, partially decoupling the suppression of unwarranted confidence from the loss of warranted confidence.

</details>


### [54] [Streaming Knowledge Compilation: Proactive Materiality-Scored Pinning for Time-Evolving LLM Wikis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09877)
*Juan M. Huerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Streaming Knowledge Compilation: A method for maintaining pre-filled KV caches in LLM wiki systems that adapt to evolving information landscapes using a materiality signal to score document importance, achieving O(√(T log K)) regret bound across domains like finance and Wikipedia.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM wiki systems assume static corpora, but real-world information evolves over time. The authors aim to handle document streams with fixed token budgets and unknown future queries by maintaining a compiled wiki that minimizes cumulative regret.

Method: Formalizes Streaming Knowledge Compilation with a materiality signal φ_t(k,n)∈[0,1] that scores document importance for entities at time t. Uses this signal as a query-relevance surrogate for proactive pinning. Proves O(√(T log K)) regret bound where ε=𝔼[|φ_t-φ̂_t|] is the only domain-specific quantity.

Result: Instantiated in two domains: finance (using abnormal stock volatility prediction with AUROC=0.728) and Wikipedia (using Abnormal Edit Ratio). Finance cumulative regret converges to -20.0, Wikipedia to +16.0. End-to-end QA on 173 finance and 119 Wikipedia pairs shows LLM-as-judge confound on post-training knowledge.

Conclusion: Regret analysis, not absolute QA scores, is the reliable evaluation metric for compiled knowledge systems. The O(√(T log K)) guarantee applies to any domain where knowledge gaps can be predicted from streaming signals. Positive regret in Wikipedia confirms Wikipedia edit content is genuinely post-training knowledge.

Abstract: LLM wiki systems compile knowledge into pre-filled KV caches for efficient inference, but assume a static corpus -- an assumption that fails whenever the underlying information landscape evolves. We formalize Streaming Knowledge Compilation: given a document stream, a fixed token budget, and future queries unknown at ingestion time, maintain a compiled wiki that minimizes cumulative regret against an offline oracle with perfect foresight. The enabling insight is a materiality signal $φ_t(k,n)\in[0,1]$ that scores document importance for entity $k$ at time $t$, acting as a query-relevance surrogate for proactive pinning before queries arrive; we prove an $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ regret bound where $\varepsilon=\mathbb{E}[|φ_t-\hatφ_t|]$ is the only domain-specific quantity. We instantiate in two domains: finance, where $φ_t$ is abnormal stock volatility predicted by frozen Llama 3.1 8B classification head (AUROC = 0.728 on 76K articles, strict temporal split; $1.49\times$ higher realized forward volatility for predicted-material articles); and Wikipedia, where $φ_t$ is the Abnormal Edit Ratio (AER), a cross-sectionally normalized edit velocity -- showing the same algorithm generalizes beyond the finance domain. End-to-end QA evaluation on 173 matched pairs (finance) and 119 (Wikipedia) reveals a pervasive LLM-as-judge confound on post-training knowledge, establishing that regret analysis -- not absolute QA scores -- is the reliable evaluation metric for compiled knowledge systems. Finance cumulative regret converges to -20.0 (-0.12/step); Wikipedia to +16.0 (+0.13/step), with the positive sign confirming that Wikipedia edit content is genuinely post-training -- richer context consistently improves scores (No Wiki 3.80 vs. Oracle 4.74) -- and eliminates this confound. The $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ guarantee applies to any domain where knowledge gaps can be predicted from streaming signals.

</details>


### [55] [Operator Fusion for LLM Inference on the Tensix Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09879)
*Qingbo Wu,Ke Li,Wenzhu Wang,Jie Yu,Ruian Zhang,Lili Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 Tenstorrent Tensix 架构上 Transformer 模型设备端推理瓶颈，提出一种增强数据局部性的算子融合策略，将 RMSNorm 与自注意力及前馈网络中的矩阵乘法融合，配合基于片上 SRAM 的连续执行及基于 NoC 的多播机制，显著减少 DRAM 读写与调度开销，并缓解多核并行下的带宽竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 模型在 Tenstorrent Tensix 架构上进行设备端推理时存在性能瓶颈，需要优化数据局部性与并行调度以提升效率。

Method: 1) 算子融合：将 RMSNorm 与自注意力及前馈网络中的矩阵乘法融合，使内存密集型与计算密集型算子在片上 SRAM 中连续执行；2) 多播机制：利用基于片上网络的行/列主节点广播输入与权重，支持多核并行并缓解 DRAM 带宽竞争。

Result: 在 Wormhole 平台上对 Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen3-0.6B 和 Qwen3-4B 进行实验：注意力层延迟降低最多达 37.44%，MLP 层降低 15.89%，每解码层平均降低 7.91%；Pearson 相关系数保持高于 98.75%，保证数值一致性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持数值一致性的前提下，显著提升了端到端推理效率，证明了算子融合与多播机制在 Tenstorrent Tensix 架构上对 Transformer 模型推理性能的有效性。

Abstract: This study addresses on-device inference bottlenecks of Transformer models on Tenstorrent's Tensix architecture and proposes an operator fusion strategy that enhances data locality. RMSNorm is fused with matrix multiplication in self-attention and in the FFN, enabling back-to-back execution of memory-bound and compute-bound operators in on-chip SRAM to significantly reduce DRAM reads/writes of intermediate results and scheduling overhead. To support multi-core parallelism, a NoC-based multicast mechanism is leveraged in which row/column master nodes efficiently distribute inputs and weights across the core mesh, alleviating DRAM bandwidth contention. Experiments on the Wormhole platform with Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen3-0.6B, and Qwen3-4B show up to 37.44% latency reduction for attention and 15.89% for MLP, with up to 7.91% reduction per decoder layer, while Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) remains above 98.75%, confirming significant end-to-end efficiency gains under numerical consistency.

</details>


### [56] [TD-Grokking: Learning from Zero-Reward Problems by Training-Time Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09883)
*Ningyuan Xi,Hao Xu,Hongsheng Xin,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TD-Grokking是一个训练时分解框架，通过将难解的零奖励问题递归分解为可验证的子问题树，将原本无法优化的零奖励样本转化为有效的训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）在推理任务中取得了显著进展，但在高度困难的零奖励问题上面临瓶颈：所有采样推理轨迹都失败，无法提供优化信号。现有的解决方案（如密集过程监督、部分奖励分配、前缀引导探索）存在固有任务约束或无法充分赋予策略模型解决原始难题的能力。

Method: TD-Grokking采用训练时分解框架，递归地将难解的根问题分解为自包含、可验证的子问题，形成分层树结构，其中可解决的叶子节点提供非零奖励。

Result: 在数学和医疗任务上的评估显示，TD-Grokking在数学和医疗任务上优于原始GRPO以及所有基线方法。结果表明训练时分解有效将零奖励示例转化为可用的训练信号，带来持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: 训练时分解能够将原本无法优化的零奖励问题转化为优化信号，使模型在困难任务上获得一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress in reasoning tasks, largely driven by post-training paradigms, especially reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, a critical bottleneck persists: RLVR fails on highly challenging zero-reward problems, where all sampled reasoning trajectories yield uniformly failed outcomes, providing no optimization signal to drive model improvement. Prior efforts to address this limitation, such as dense process supervision, partial reward assignment, or prefix-guided exploration, suffer from inherent task constraints or do not fully equip the policy model with the capabilities necessary to solve the original intractable problems. To address this, we propose TD-Grokking, a training-time decomposition framework for zero-reward problems. It recursively decomposes intractable root problems into self-contained, verifiable subproblems, forming hierarchical trees where solvable leaves provide non-zero rewards. Evaluations on mathematical and medical tasks show that TD-Grokking outperforms vanilla GRPO as well as all baseline approaches. Together with detailed analysis, these results confirm that training-time decomposition effectively converts zero-reward examples into usable training signals, enabling consistent performance gains. Our code and datasets are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TD-Grokking-6567/.

</details>


### [57] [TENP: Trapezoidal Expert Neuron Pruning For Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09885)
*Jiangyang He,Shaolin Zhu,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TENP框架，通过专家神经元剪枝在保持模型性能的同时减少MoE-LLM的静态参数占用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE-LLM因静态参数占用大而部署受限。现有方法要么移除整个专家破坏路由拓扑，要么依赖非结构化剪枝效率有限

Method: 基于少量样本识别保留重要专家，对次要专家应用专家神经元剪枝，在浅层到深层呈梯形保留参数。评估专家重要性时联合考虑输出幅度和改变输入向量方向的能力；ENP中通过测量神经元对专家输出的投影贡献来筛选重要神经元

Result: 在Qwen和DeepSeek模型上验证。路由专家稀疏度40%、平均激活63.76%参数时，DeepSeek仅损失1点准确率，且在代码生成任务上比全参模型提升10%

Conclusion: TENP有效减少参数占用，同时保持或提升模型性能

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts large language models (LLMs) scale efficiently through sparse activation, yet their deployment is fundamentally constrained by the large static parameter footprint of experts. Existing compression approaches either remove entire experts, disrupting routing topology and harming performance, or rely on unstructured weight pruning with limited practical efficiency. To address the limitations, we propose TENP, a structured Trapezoidal ExpertNeuron Pruning framework. Using a few samples, we identify and retain important experts, while applying expert neuron pruning (ENP) to less important experts, reserving model parameters in a trapezoidal pattern from shallow to deep layers. When evaluating expert importance, we jointly consider both the magnitude of the expert output and its ability to change the direction of the input vector. For ENP, we measure each neuron's projected contribution to the expert output to identify and retain important neurons. We conduct extensive experiments on the Qwen and DeepSeek models. Under a routing expert sparsity of 40% and an average of 63.76% activated expert parameters, the DeepSeek model suffers only a 1-point drop in accuracy compared to the full-parameter model. Moreover, it outperforms the full-parameter model by 10% on code generation tasks.

</details>


### [58] [SHAPE: Coalition-Aware Expert Pruning for Sparse Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09886)
*Yuhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHAPE是一种基于博弈论的任务驱动MoE专家剪枝框架，通过Shapley值建模专家合作，在20%-40%剪枝率下保持性能并降低GPU内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE大模型存在内存墙问题（需保留所有专家以支持路由），现有专家剪枝方法独立评分专家，忽视了MoE推理的协作本质（输出源于top-k专家组合），导致剪枝效果不佳。

Method: 1) 将路由轨迹建模为合作博弈；2) 通过Shapley风格归因分配交互感知专家价值；3) 质量覆盖选择规则保留覆盖α比例正Shapley质量的最小专家子集；4) 使用二分法匹配目标保留率。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B、GPT-OSS-20B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite上验证，SHAPE在20%-40%专家剪枝下保持竞争力精度，优于全局/逐层剪枝，无需额外训练，显著降低GPU峰值内存。

Conclusion: SHAPE通过显式建模专家协作识别关键专家，在保持MoE拓扑结构的同时实现高效剪枝，为MoE模型部署提供内存优化方案。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models achieve strong quality with low per-token compute, yet their deployment is often limited by the memory wall: the full expert pool must remain resident to support token-dependent routing. Expert pruning is a direct remedy, but prior criteria typically score experts independently and overlook that MoE inference is inherently \emph{coalitional}, where outputs arise from routed top-$k$ expert combinations. We propose \textbf{SHAPE}, a task-driven pruning framework that explicitly models \emph{intra-layer} expert cooperation. SHAPE formulates routing traces on a small calibration set as an empirical cooperative game and assigns interaction-aware expert values via a Shapley-style attribution over observed top-$k$ coalitions, enabling the identification of experts that are essential for high-utility collaborations rather than merely frequent. To preserve MoE topology under a global pruning budget, SHAPE further introduces a \emph{quality-coverage} selection rule that retains, in each layer, the minimal expert subset covering an $α$ fraction of non-negative Shapley mass, while using bisection to match a target keep rate. Experiments on three modern MoE backbones (Qwen3-30B-A3B, GPT-OSS-20B, and DeepSeek-V2-Lite) across diverse benchmarks show that SHAPE consistently improves robustness over global and layer-wise pruning variants, maintaining competitive accuracy under 20\% and 40\% expert pruning without additional training and delivering clear reductions in peak GPU memory footprint. The open-source code is available at https://github.com/Alizen-1009/Shapley-Moe.

</details>


### [59] [SocraticPO: Policy Optimization via Interactive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09887)
*Zirui Liu,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu,Xianquan Wang,Jiayu Liu,Tingyue Pan,Qingchuan Li,Jing Sha,Zhenya Huang,Shijin Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SocraticPO通过在强化学习rollout中引入苏格拉底式自然语言指导（学生独立作答，错误时教师诊断并提供简短修正指导，学生继续作答），并配合奖励衰减（教师介入后的正确回答仅获得衰减奖励），在保留标准期望奖励目标的同时，有效避免模型过度依赖教师帮助，在SciKnowEval本科生科学推理基准上优于现有RL和自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL通常使用标量结果奖励（如二元正确性），虽提供优化方向但很少解释如何修正错误推理，容易导致捷径学习和脆弱策略；因此需要一种既能提供推理指导又能防止过度依赖教师帮助的方法。

Method: SocraticPO框架在RL rollout中引入苏格拉底式自然语言指导：学生先独立作答；若错误，教师诊断并提供简短修正指导；学生继续在扩展上下文中作答；关键在于配合奖励衰减（教师介入后的正确回答仅获得衰减奖励），使其可插拔到现有策略梯度后端（如Reinforce++），且仅需文本级指导，无需访问logits或分布匹配。

Result: 在SciKnowEval的本科生科学推理基准上，SocraticPO优于强基线RL和自蒸馏方法；消融实验表明，针对性指导和奖励衰减均不可或缺，且奖励衰减有效缓解了模型对辅助修正的依赖。

Conclusion: SocraticPO通过在rollout中嵌入苏格拉底式自然语言指导和奖励衰减，在不改变标准期望奖励目标的前提下，既提供了推理修正指引又避免了模型过度依赖教师帮助，证明了该方法在科学推理任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models usually supervises reasoning with scalar outcome rewards, such as binary correctness. Such rewards provide an optimization direction but rarely explain how a model should revise its mistaken reasoning, which can encourage shortcut learning and brittle policies. We propose \textbf{SocraticPO} (Socratic Policy Optimization), a policy-optimization framework that augments RL rollouts with Socratic-style natural-language guidance. During rollout, the student first answers independently; if the answer is incorrect, a teacher diagnoses the attempt and provides concise corrective guidance, after which the student continues under the expanded context. Crucially, this guidance is paired with reward decay: correct answers obtained after teacher intervention only receive decayed rewards, preventing the policy from treating teacher help as a free path to reward. Since SocraticPO only modifies the rollout process while leaving the standard expected-reward objective intact, it can be plugged into existing policy-gradient backends such as Reinforce++. Moreover, because the teacher provides only text-level guidance, SocraticPO can leverage stronger black-box teacher models without requiring access to logits or distribution matching. On undergraduate-level scientific reasoning benchmarks from SciKnowEval, SocraticPO improves over strong RL and self-distillation baselines. Ablations show that both targeted guidance and reward decay are necessary, with reward decay mitigating reliance on assisted correction.

</details>


### [60] [PreAct-Bench: Benchmarking Predictive Monitoring in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09890)
*Hainiu Xu,Italo Luis da Silva,Jiangnan Ye,Yuhao Wang,Wei Liu,Linyi Yang,Jonathan Richard Schwarz,Nicola Paoletti,Yulan He,Hanqi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM安全领域的新任务"预测性监控"，即在行动执行前根据部分轨迹预测是否会演变为不道德行为，并构建了包含1000对轨迹的PreActBench基准。研究显示即使对强大模型，预测性监控仍极具挑战性，凸显了LLM安全中前瞻性风险推理的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全研究主要关注对完整行动轨迹中不道德行为的检测，这种范式本质上是回顾性的，即在伤害已经发生后才识别危害。研究指出这一方法存在局限，因为无法在有害行为实际执行前进行干预，因此需要一种前瞻性的安全监控方法。

Method: 提出了"预测性监控"任务：给定部分行动轨迹，预测是否会演变为不道德行为。构建了PreActBench基准，包含1000对跨五个领域的道德/不道德行动轨迹。使用Prefix Foresight F1指标评估多种LLM、安全护栏模型和潜在探测方法在不同轨迹片段上的表现。

Result: 人类在预测性监控任务中表现出有希望的性能，但即使是强大模型也难以完成这一任务。这表明预测性监控仍极具挑战性，凸显了在LLM安全研究中需要更强的前瞻性风险推理能力。

Conclusion: 预测性监控是LLM安全领域一个关键但常被忽视的任务，现有LLM在预测有害行为方面的能力仍有限，未来需要在LLM安全研究中加强前瞻性风险推理方法的发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents capable of executing multi-step action trajectories toward a given objective. While existing safety research has focused on detecting unethical behavior from complete trajectories, this paradigm is fundamentally retrospective: it identifies harm only after it has already occurred. In this work, we study a critical yet overlooked safety task, which we term Predictive Monitoring: given only a partial action trajectory, can a model infer whether it will culminate in an unethical action before the overt action is executed? To support this task, we present PreActBench, a benchmark of 1,000 paired ethical and unethical action trajectories spanning five domains. We evaluate a range of LLMs, safety guardrail models, and latent probing methods across varying fractions of the action trajectory using our Prefix Foresight F1 metric. Results show that while humans achieve promising performance, predictive monitoring remains challenging even for strong models, highlighting the need for future-oriented risk reasoning in LLM safety.

</details>


### [61] [IntentKV: Cross-Turn Intent-Aware KV Cache Pruning for Agent Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09916)
*Junjie Li,Jiong Lou,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IntentKV是一种学习的KV剪枝方法，通过会话级QueryMemory和记忆注意力规则，在保持基础LLM冻结的同时，显著减少长轨迹多轮对话中的KV缓存占用，缓解KV缓存而非参数计算成为主要瓶颈的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM代理在长时域运行时，工具调用、搜索结果和中间推理会产生海量KV记忆和KV读取带宽（呈数量级增长），导致KV缓存成为服务瓶颈而非参数计算，需要优化KV存储和访问效率。

Method: 1. 保持基础LLM参数冻结；2. 维护会话级QueryMemory存储跨轮次意图；3. 用记忆注意力规则对实时历史令牌评分；4. 添加零初始化残差头，对当前查询K-V向量做交叉注意力；5. 采用槽位映射重定向驱逐策略，确保与前缀缓存兼容（被丢弃位置路由到哨兵死槽，存活K/V行、RoPE相位和槽位身份保持不变）。

Result: 在8k KV预算下，Qwen3-8B平均峰值请求令牌减少23.9%，Qwen2.5-14B减少30.7%；在最长的100个BCP查询上，峰值请求令牌从92.3k降至20.5k（减少77.8%），原始KV读取从411M降至31M（减少92.6%），几乎不损失准确性。

Conclusion: IntentKV在紧约束KV预算下匹配无剪枝全缓存基线的准确性，通过高效KV剪枝大幅降低长轨迹代理的KV存储和读取开销，是解决KV缓存瓶颈的有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-turn LLM agents fan short queries into long trajectories of tool calls, search results, and intermediate reasoning. Both KV memory and KV read bandwidth grow by orders of magnitude across a single trajectory, making the key-value (KV) cache, not parameter compute, the dominant serving bottleneck for long-horizon agents. We introduce IntentKV, learned KV pruning that keeps the base LLM frozen. IntentKV maintains a session-level QueryMemory of cross-turn intent, scores live history tokens with a memory-attention rule, and adds a zero-initialized residual head with cross-attention over current-query K-vectors. To stay composable with prefix caches, eviction is a slot-map redirection: dropped positions route to a sentinel dead slot while surviving K/V rows, RoPE phases, and slot identities stay in place. IntentKV matches the no-pruning full-cache baseline with almost no accuracy drop under tight KV budgets: at an 8k KV budget, mean peak request tokens drop 23.9% on Qwen3-8B and 30.7% on Qwen2.5-14B. On the 100 longest BCP queries that all methods complete on Qwen2.5-14B, IntentKV-8k further cuts worst-case peak request tokens from 92.3k to 20.5k, a 77.8% reduction, and worst-case raw KV reads from 411M to 31M, a 92.6% reduction.

</details>


### [62] [When RL Fails after SFT: Rejuvenating Model Plasticity for Robust SFT-to-RL Handoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09932)
*Runze Liu,Jiashun Liu,Xu Wan,Yuqian Fu,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对过度监督微调（SFT）导致模型在强化学习阶段性能提升受限的问题，提出Rejuvenation方法，通过基模型锚定融合和神经元重置来恢复模型可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）后接强化学习（RL）是LLM后训练的标准流程，但过度SFT的模型在RL阶段往往性能提升有限。作者认为问题根源在于SFT导致的模型可塑性丧失：SFT初始化的策略难以被后续RL有效重塑。

Method: 提出Rejuvenation方法，通过两个关键步骤：1）基模型锚定融合（base-anchored model fusion）减少过度SFT引起的漂移；2）目标神经元重置（targeted neuron reset）缓解模型刚性。该方法在保留SFT获得的有用先验的同时恢复模型可塑性。

Result: 在数学推理任务和智能体任务上，Rejuvenation在过度训练的SFT模型上持续提升RL性能，同时增强对分布外任务的泛化能力。分析表明过度SFT模型倾向于产生过度自信的token分布和尖锐的参数景观，使得RL优化更困难。

Conclusion: 过度SFT会损害模型的参数景观和输出分布，降低RL优化难度。Rejuvenation通过基模型融合和神经元重置有效恢复了模型可塑性，证明了简单方法在改善SFT到RL过渡中的有效性。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) followed by Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become a standard pipeline for Large Language Model (LLM) post-training. SFT is expected to provide a useful behavioral prior for RL to further enhance model capabilities. However, checkpoints with excessive SFT often show limited improvement during RL. We attribute this failure to the loss of model plasticity: the reduced ability of an SFT-initialized policy to be effectively reshaped by subsequent RL. To better understand this phenomenon, we conduct detailed analysis from multiple perspectives, including parameter changes, output spaces, and RL optimization dynamics. Our results show that models from excessive SFT tend to produce over-confident token distributions and exhibit sharp parameter landscapes, which make them harder to optimize in the RL stage. To enable a more robust SFT-to-RL handoff, we propose \texttt{Rejuvenation}, a simple yet effective method that restores plasticity while preserving useful SFT-acquired priors. Rejuvenation leverages base-anchored model fusion to reduce excessive SFT-induced drift with targeted neuron reset to mitigate model rigidity. Experimental results on both math reasoning tasks and agentic tasks demonstrate that our approach consistently improves RL performance on over-trained SFT models, while also enhancing generalization to out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>

*Anirudh Sekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We introduce RKSC (Reasoning-Aware KV Cache Sharing), a training-free inference framework that eliminates two structural redundancies in multi-branch LLM reasoning pipelines. ASKS (Attention-Similarity KV Sharing) computes the prefix KV cache once and broadcasts it to all semantically similar branches via hidden-state cosine similarity, strictly generalising the token-exact prefix caching used by vLLM and SGLang. CGEE (Confidence-Gated Early Exit) applies two complementary exit mechanisms: (1) it skips the verification forward pass entirely when generation confidence is decisive across branches, and (2) it terminates the verification pass at an intermediate layer when per-layer entropy stabilises, using lightweight hooks on the transformer backbone. RSBCM (Reasoning-Selective Block Cache Manager) prevents unbounded cache growth via attention-weighted depth-priority eviction. Across five model families (7B-10B), four benchmarks, and 1,000 evaluated problems, RKSC achieves a mean speedup of 3.008x over the No-KV baseline (peak 3.990x), a 1.66x mean improvement over vLLM-equivalent prefix caching, with a CGEE-induced error rate of only 0.37% (6 errors out of 1,616 verify calls). No fine-tuning or architecture changes are required. Code is available at https://github.com/AnirudhSekar/RKSC.

</details>


### [64] [Baseline-Free Policy Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10321)
*Carlos S. Sepúlveda,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在神经组合优化（NCO）中采用无基线的GRPO算法，通过在采样轨迹组内归一化优势函数，避免了REINFORCE基线方法在困难实例上导致的训练崩溃，在TSP和CVRP基准测试中与POMO等强基线方法相比性能相当且无需外部基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化的标准训练算法REINFORCE使用基线来降低方差，但在困难实例中，基线不足会产生噪声梯度估计，导致训练不稳定。作者旨在解决这一结构性脆弱性问题。

Method: 引入来自大语言模型对齐领域的GRPO算法，通过在采样轨迹组内归一化优势函数完全消除基线。在RL4CO框架下，与五种强化学习算法在TSP和CVRP基准上进行对比实验。

Result: GRPO避免了REINFORCE在TSP-100上的训练崩溃（成本从9.8恶化到52.1）；在匹配梯度更新的情况下，GRPO与POMO（强AM多起始基线）的解质量仅相差2%，且无需外部基线；P3O在TSP上表现竞争，但在CVRP上变异性较高。

Conclusion: GRPO是无基线NCO的有前景替代方案，特别适用于基线依赖训练变得脆弱的场景，证明了消除基线不仅可行而且能提升训练稳定性。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) trains autoregressive policies to solve routing problems. The standard training algorithm, REINFORCE with a rollout baseline, requires maintaining and periodically updating a frozen copy of the policy for variance reduction. This baseline introduces a structural vulnerability: on harder instances, a poor baseline produces noisy gradient estimates that can destabilize training. We evaluate Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), an algorithm from large language model alignment that eliminates the baseline entirely by normalizing advantages within groups of sampled trajectories. In a controlled comparison of five RL algorithms on TSP and CVRP benchmarks within the RL4CO framework, we find that: (i) GRPO avoids the training collapse observed with REINFORCE on TSP-100, where performance degrades from cost 9.8 to 52.1 immediately after the warmup phase and does not recover under extended training; (ii) at matched gradient updates, GRPO achieves solution quality within 2% of POMO, a strong AM-based multi-start baseline, while requiring no external baseline; and (iii) P3O, a pairwise preference algorithm also from the alignment literature, is competitive on TSP but shows higher variability on CVRP. These results identify GRPO as a promising baseline-free alternative for NCO, particularly in settings where baseline-dependent training becomes fragile.

</details>


### [65] [3SPO: State-Score-Supervised Policy Optimization for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09961)
*Yu Han,Kailing Li,Yang Jiao,Yulin Dai,Yuqian Fu,Linhai Zhuo,Tianwen Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为3SPO（State-Score-Supervised Policy Optimization）的新型强化学习算法，通过动态状态分数监督实现步后策略优化，解决了LLM智能体在稀疏奖励设置中的信用分配问题。在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验显示，3SPO比GRPO性能提升22.6%和15.6点，同时实现2.4倍更多状态探索和1.8倍更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体训练的RL算法在轨迹级别优化，导致在多轮交互、稀疏奖励和延迟奖励场景下信用分配困难。需要一种细粒度的步后优化方法，在每个步骤进行策略更新，而无需完整轨迹收集。

Method: State-Score-Supervised Policy Optimization (3SPO)算法：在每个步骤基于历史成功率计算状态分数，实现步后策略优化；无需价值函数估计或辅助模型；在单状态带状抽象下提供理论保证，包括对数分配遗憾和样本复杂度保证。

Result: 在ALFWorld上比GRPO提升22.6%，在WebShop上提升15.6点；使用相同资源实现2.4倍更多状态探索和1.8倍更快收敛；理论上在单状态带状抽象下实现对数分配遗憾和样本复杂度保证。

Conclusion: 3SPO是一种有效的LLM智能体训练方法，能够解决稀疏奖励设置下的信用分配问题。该方法在理论上有坚实的保证，在实验中表现优异，为训练高性能的LLM智能体提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) as autonomous agents via reinforcement learning (RL) has enabled frontier models to achieve superhuman performance in long-horizon tasks. However, existing RL algorithms operate at the trajectory level, performing policy optimization only after collecting complete episode rollouts. This coarse-grained approach faces fundamental challenges in multi-turn agent settings where rewards are sparse, delayed, and credit assignment across individual steps is critical. In this work, we propose \textbf{State-Score-Supervised Policy Optimization (3SPO)}, a novel RL algorithm that performs post-step policy optimization with dynamic state score supervision. At each step, 3SPO computes the state score based on historical success rates, supervising step-wise credit assignment, adaptive rollout and post-step policy optimization without requiring value function estimation or additional auxiliary models. Theoretically, under a per-state bandit abstraction, we show that the proposed score-supervised allocation mechanism achieves logarithmic allocation regret and provide sample-complexity guarantees for action identification, score distinguishability, and filtering stability. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B-Instruct show that 3SPO consistently outperforms GRPO by $+22.6\%$ on ALFWorld and $+15.6$ points on WebShop, while using comparable resources to achieve $2.4\times$ more state exploration and $1.8\times$ faster convergence. Code is available at https://github.com/genalyu/3SPO.

</details>


### [66] [Bittensor Agent Arenas as a Trajectory Primitive: Distilling a Shopping Agent from ShoppingBench Subnet Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10064)
*Shardul Bansal,Seth Schilbe,Jarrod Barnes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过构建激励对齐的代理竞技场（ORO Subnet 15）来生成高质量轨迹数据，用于小模型的强化学习微调。实验表明，该方法能将Qwen3-4B的ASR从18.0%提升至42.7%，接近合成数据SFT基线的43.6%，且训练成本仅为单日子网输出量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小模型代理后训练受限于轨迹数据质量而非算法本身。现有数据源存在两个主要问题：frontier合成数据继承合成器的偏差且长尾坍缩；未过滤的生产日志未经评判且被捷径行为污染。因此需要一种能生成激励对齐、可评判且抗记忆化的高质量轨迹数据的机制。

Method: 作者在Bittensor上部署ORO Subnet 15（基于ShoppingBench），利用竞赛机制、LLM推理裁判和轮换泄漏-集群保护的问题集，生成包含激励对齐多样性、轨迹级评判和抗记忆化评估的数据。通过结构质量过滤器保留模型自主发出工具调用的代理轨迹，剔除仅分类或叙述确定性搜索循环的子任务轨迹。在Qwen3-4B上采用与ShoppingBench SFT-then-GRPO相匹配的配方进行微调。

Result: 在泄漏-集群保护的测试集上，Qwen3-4B的ASR从基线18.0%提升至42.7%，仅与合成数据SFT基线（43.6%）有单问题噪声差距，且训练仅用单日子网输出。pass@8到pass@1的差距为53.3% vs 34.8%，通过每步教师引导的Dr. GRPO奖励将剩余空间转化为过程改进，发现子任务火海是缩小与SFT+GRPO基线（48.7%）差距的主要杠杆。

Conclusion: 激励对齐的代理竞技场可有效生成高质量轨迹数据，突破小模型代理后训练的瓶颈。实验证明了该方法在ASR提升、训练效率和过程改进方面的有效性，并开源了过滤器、数据集划分和竞技场机制，为社区提供了可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: Small-model agentic post-training is bottlenecked less by the algorithm than by the trajectory substrate it consumes. Leading recipes (RLVR, group-relative RL, rejection-sampled re-SFT) all need multi-turn traces carrying per-trajectory supervision, and the two existing sources fall short: frontier-synthesised data inherits the synthesizer's biases and collapses the long tail, while unfiltered production logs are unjudged and contaminated by shortcut behaviour. We argue that an incentive-aligned agent arena can be engineered to manufacture such trajectories, and demonstrate this on ORO Subnet 15 (SN15), a Bittensor deployment of the ShoppingBench agentic-commerce benchmark. SN15's race mechanism, LLM reasoning judge, and rotating leak-cluster-guarded problem suite yield a corpus with three properties: incentive-aligned diversity, per-trajectory judging, and anti-memorised held-out evaluation. We introduce a structural-quality filter that converts the raw firehose into a trainable corpus by keeping agentic trajectories (the model itself emits the tool calls) and rejecting sub-task trajectories (the model only classifies or narrates over a deterministic search loop), then post-train Qwen3-4B with a recipe matched to the published ShoppingBench SFT-then-GRPO pipeline. On a leak-cluster-guarded held-out partition scored production-strict, the model lifts from the published Qwen3-4B base of 18.0% ASR to 42.7%, within single-problem noise of the synthetic-data SFT-only baseline (43.6%), while training on a fraction of a single day of subnet output. The supervised stack leaves a large pass@8 to pass@1 gap (53.3% vs 34.8%); a per-step teacher-grounded Dr. GRPO reward converts that headroom into process improvement, and we identify the sub-task firehose as the primary lever for closing the gap to the 48.7% SFT+GRPO bar. We release the filter, the corpus splits, and the arena mechanics.

</details>


### [67] [Unsupervised Style Representation Learning for AI-Text Detection via Paraphrase Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10099)
*Rafael Rivera Soto,Barry Chen,Nicholas Andrews*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需作者标签的机器生成文本检测方法。通过训练风格编码器从机器改写文本中重建人类文本，该方法在少样本检测任务中超越了所有基线，在零样本设置下与监督分类器表现相当且泛化能力更强，并且还能迁移到作者验证和细粒度风格区分等未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的快速发展引发了抄袭、虚假信息和自动化影响操作等滥用担忧，促使对鲁棒检测器的需求。现有基于风格的检测器依赖作者标签训练，且仅限于少样本推理，需要分布内样本，存在局限性。

Method: 训练一个风格编码器，使其从机器生成的改写文本中重建人类原创文本。训练时冻结语义编码器，使风格编码器专注于捕捉用于重建的非语义特征。通过两种检测策略评估学习到的表示：少样本检测器和零样本DeepSVDD检测器。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，该方法在少样本设置中匹配或超越所有基线；在零样本情况下，与分布内测试数据的全监督分类器表现相当，且对未见LLM的泛化能力更强；学习到的表示还能泛化到未见任务，在作者验证和细粒度风格区分任务上取得竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 无需作者标签的风格表示学习能够有效检测机器生成文本，并且具有良好的泛化能力，不仅适用于检测任务，还能迁移到其他相关任务，为LLM滥用检测提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has raised concerns about misuse such as plagiarism, misinformation, and automated influence operations, motivating the need for robust detectors. Recent work has shown that neural representations of writing style are effective for detection and, crucially, robust to adversarial attacks that defeat most existing detectors. However, current style-based detectors rely on authorship labels for training, and are limited to few-shot inference for detection, requiring in-distribution samples that may not always be available. We learn discriminative style features without authorship labels by training a style encoder to reconstruct human-authored text from its machine-generated paraphrase; freezing a semantic encoder during training biases the style encoder to capture only the non-semantic features needed for reconstruction. We evaluate the learned representations via two detection strategies: a few-shot detector and a zero-shot DeepSVDD-based detector. Across benchmarks, our method matches or outperforms all baselines in the few-shot setting and, in the zero-shot regime, is competitive with fully supervised classifiers on in-distribution test data while generalizing better to unseen LLMs. Beyond detection, the learned representations generalize to unseen tasks, achieving competitive performance on authorship verification and fine-grained style discrimination despite never being trained on either objective.

</details>


### [68] [Dropout-GRPO: Variational Stochasticity for Continuous Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10184)
*Wooil Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GRPO算法在连续潜在推理模型中因轨迹确定性导致的多样性不足问题，作者提出通过结构化Dropout注入随机性，使每个rollout作为参数变分分布的后验样本，从而实现基于贝叶斯模型平均策略的GRPO优化。在GSM8K数据集上，该方法使Coconut基线模型的pass@1从27.29%提升至29.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO算法依赖每个组内K个rollout的多样性来计算组平均优势A^(k)=r^(k)-μ_r，否则该值会坍缩为零。对于Coconut等连续潜在推理模型，其通过连续隐藏状态递归替代离散思维链token，由于给定参数和提示时潜在阶段本质上是确定性的，多次rollout会产生相同轨迹，导致GRPO进度停滞。

Method: 提出通过结构化Dropout注入必要的随机性：对每个rollout应用单个在所有潜在递归步骤中保持恒定的伯努利mask，从而生成必要的轨迹方差。这种共享mask将每个rollout视为参数变分分布的后验样本，使GRPO能够优化贝叶斯模型平均策略的期望奖励。

Result: 在GSM8K数学推理数据集上，dropout-GRPO方法将Coconut基线模型的pass@1准确率从27.29%提升至29.01%，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为潜在推理LLM的后训练提供了一种实用且理论上扎实的方法，证明了GRPO学习在连续潜在推理模型中的可行性。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) relies on the diversity of $K$ rollouts within each group; otherwise, the group-mean advantage $A^{(k)} = r^{(k)} - μ_r$ collapses to zero. This presents a structural challenge for latent-reasoning models like Coconut, which feed continuous hidden states recurrently in place of discrete chain-of-thought tokens. Because the latent phase is inherently deterministic given the parameters and prompt, multiple rollouts produce identical trajectories, stalling GRPO's progress. Consequently, applying group-relative reinforcement learning to continuous latent reasoning has proven difficult.
  To address this, we propose sourcing the necessary stochasticity through structured dropout. By applying a single Bernoulli mask held constant across all latent recurrence steps for a given rollout, we generate essential trajectory variance. This shared mask effectively treats each rollout as a posterior sample from a variational distribution over parameters, allowing GRPO to optimize the expected reward of a Bayesian model-average policy. We provide both theoretical justification for this method -- including unbiasedness, variance reduction, and the well-definedness of the latent gradient -- and empirical validation. On GSM8K, dropout-GRPO improves a Coconut baseline from $27.29\%$ to $29.01\%$ pass@1, demonstrating the viability of GRPO learning for latent-reasoning models. Our work positions this as a practical, theoretically grounded approach for post-training latent-reasoning LLMs.

</details>


### [69] [SpenseGPT: Practical One-shot Pruning Enabling Sparse and Dense GEMMs for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10445)
*Jaeseong Lee,Seung-won Hwang,Samyam Rajbhandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spense混合稀疏-密集格式及SpenseGPT一阶段后训练剪枝方法，在保持精度的同时实现LLM解码速度提升1.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化2:4稀疏性虽提供理论2倍加速，但严格的50%稀疏性约束常导致精度下降；现有放宽稀疏性格式要么需专门编译器支持，要么引入运行时开销，限制端到端加速。

Method: 提出Spense混合稀疏-密集格式，将权重矩阵分为2:4稀疏区域和密集区域；基于此设计SpenseGPT一阶段后训练剪枝方法，采用两种策略选择密集区域，保持与现有稀疏和密集GEMM库兼容，无需定制编译器和输入激活扩展。

Result: 在Qwen3-32B和Seed-OSS-36B上的实验表明，该方法在B200 GPU的FP8精度下实现高达1.2倍端到端解码速度提升，同时保持模型精度。

Conclusion: 首次在B200等现代GPU的半结构化稀疏张量核上实现现实世界端到端LLM解码速度提升的一阶段剪枝演示，兼顾模型质量和加速效果。

Abstract: Semi-structured 2:4 sparsity is widely supported by modern accelerators, providing up to a 2x theoretical speedup. However, its strict 50% sparsity constraint often causes non-negligible accuracy degradation under post-training pruning. Meanwhile, existing relaxed sparsity formats either require specialized compiler support or introduce runtime overheads that limit end-to-end speedup. We propose Spense, a practical hybrid sparse-dense format that splits each weight matrix into a 2:4 sparse region and a dense region. This design relaxes the effective sparsity constraint while remaining compatible with existing high-performance sparse and dense GEMM libraries, avoiding both custom compiler support and input activation expansion. Building on this format, we introduce SpenseGPT, a one-shot post-training pruning method that produces sparse and dense regions. Notably, we show that selecting the right dense regions is important, and we devise two different strategies to choose them. Experiments on Qwen3-32B and Seed-OSS-36B demonstrate that our method achieves up to 1.2x end-to-end decoding speedup on B200 GPUs with FP8 precision, while preserving accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first one-shot pruning demonstration of real-world end-to-end LLM decoding speedup from semi-structured sparse tensor cores on recent GPUs such as B200s, while maintaining model quality.

</details>


### [70] [Advancing the State-of-the-Art in Empirical Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10481)
*Nicole Mitchell,Galen Andrew,Arun Ganesh,Brendan McMahan,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于合成“canary”样本的参数高效微调大语言模型隐私审计方法，包括训练数据审计和合成数据审计两种机制，并系统研究了模型容量与canary熵对记忆效应的交互影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调LLM可能存在对训练样本的异常记忆问题，需要实证隐私审计来量化数据泄露风险。关键挑战在于设计能够有效嵌入敏感训练数据的“canary”样本。

Method: 1) 使用高温度采样(T≥0.8)从LLM生成针对隐私敏感数据的合成canary样本，作为高影响力异常值；2) 设计基于辅助模型微调的合成数据审计方法；3) 系统研究模型容量与canary熵对记忆效应的交互作用。

Result: 通过强审计方法成功量化了训练数据和合成数据生成过程中的隐私泄露风险，完成了模型容量与canary熵交互效应的系统研究。

Conclusion: 提出的合成canary生成方法和合成数据审计框架为LLM隐私微调提供了强有力的审计手段，能够有效检测和量化模型记忆风险，同时canary样本非私密且可重复插入，不损害真实数据隐私。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) can exhibit problematic memorization of individual training examples. Empirical privacy auditing (EPA) quantifies this risk by measuring realistic data leakage on membership inference (MI) or reconstruction attacks. A key challenge in EPA is designing ``canary'' examples that are mixed with the privacy-sensitive training data. We propose generating synthetic canaries via high-temperature sampling ($T \geq 0.8$) from LLMs, using prompts tailored to the privacy-sensitive training data. These canaries act as high-influence outliers, ensuring high identifiability and hence strong audits. Further, since the canaries are themselves non-private, they are inspectable and can be inserted with repetition without jeopardizing the privacy of the real data. An important use of models fine-tuned on privacy-sensitive data is the generation of synthetic data. This also comes with privacy risk. We introduce a powerful synthetic data audit based on fine-tuning an auxiliary model on the synthetic data. Auditing the auxiliary model for the original canaries then provides a strong estimate of the privacy leakage through the synthetic data. Finally, leveraging our strong auditing methodologies, we perform a systematic investigation into the interacting effects of model capacity and canary entropy on memorization.

</details>


### [71] [Representation-Aware Advantage Estimation: Your Reward Model Provides More Than A Scalar Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10528)
*Guozheng Li,Xiyan Fu,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed
